Implement the following plan:

# Fix Gemini Transcript Parsing for Checkpointing

## Context

Gemini checkpointing is broken because `GeminiMessage.Content` is defined as `string`, but actual Gemini CLI transcripts use different formats for the `content` field:
- **User messages**: `"content": [{"text": "the prompt"}]` (array of objects)
- **Gemini messages**: `"content": "response text"` (string)

`json.Unmarshal` fails when encountering an array where a string is expected, causing `ParseTranscript()` to return an error. This cascades through the entire checkpoint pipeline:

1. `countTranscriptItems()` returns 0 for Gemini transcripts
2. `prepare-commit-msg` hook sees "no content to link" and skips adding the `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer
3. `post-commit` hook sees no trailer and skips condensation
4. Session data is never condensed to `entire/checkpoints/v1`

All existing tests use simplified `"content": "string"` format that doesn't match the real Gemini CLI output.

## Fix

### Step 1: Add custom `UnmarshalJSON` to `GeminiMessage`

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go`

Add a custom unmarshaler that handles both content formats:
- If `content` is a string: use directly
- If `content` is an array of `[{"text": "..."}]`: extract text parts and join with newlines
- If `content` is absent/null: leave as empty string

This is the cleanest approach because all existing code accessing `msg.Content` continues to work without changes.

### Step 2: Add tests for the real Gemini transcript format

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript_test.go`

Add test cases using the actual Gemini transcript format with array-based content for user messages. Tests should cover:
- `ParseTranscript` with mixed content types (array for user, string for gemini)
- `ExtractAllUserPrompts` with array-based user content
- `ExtractLastUserPrompt` with array-based user content
- `ExtractModifiedFiles` with real Gemini transcript format
- `countTranscriptItems` in `manual_commit_condensation.go` returning correct count

### Step 3: Update existing test fixtures

**Files**: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript_test.go`, `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini_test.go`

Update existing test data to include at least some tests with the real array format to prevent regression.

### Step 4: Add a strategy-level regression test

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` tests or new test

Add a test that reproduces the original bug: `sessionHasNewContent` with a Gemini transcript containing array-content user messages should return `true`, not `false`.

## Verification

```bash
mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
```

Specifically verify:
- `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/... -run TestParseTranscript` passes with new array-content format
- `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/strategy/... -run TestSessionHasNewContent` passes with Gemini transcripts
- All existing tests still pass (the fix is backward-compatible with string content)


If you need specific details from before exiting plan mode (like exact code snippets, error messages, or content you generated), read the full transcript at: /Users/alex/.REDACTED.jsonl

```
     1→package geminicli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"

    10→
    11→// Transcript parsing types - Gemini CLI uses JSON format for session storage
    12→// Based on transcript_path format: ~/.gemini/tmp/<hash>/chats/session-<date>-<id>.json
    13→
    14→// Message type constants for Gemini transcripts
    15→const (
    16→	MessageTypeUser   = "user"
    17→	MessageTypeGemini = "gemini"
    18→)
    19→
    20→// GeminiTranscript represents the top-level structure of a Gemini session file
    21→type GeminiTranscript struct {
    22→	Messages []GeminiMessage `json:"messages"`
    23→}
    24→
    25→// GeminiMessage represents a single message in the transcript
    26→type GeminiMessage struct {
    27→	ID        string           `json:"id,omitempty"` // UUID for the message
    28→	Type      string           `json:"type"`         // MessageTypeUser or MessageTypeGemini
    29→	Content   string           `json:"content,omitempty"`
    30→	ToolCalls []GeminiToolCall `json:"toolCalls,omitempty"`
    31→}
    32→
    33→// GeminiToolCall represents a tool call in a gemini message
    34→type GeminiToolCall struct {
    35→	ID     string                 `json:"id"`
    36→	Name   string                 `json:"name"`
    37→	Args   map[string]interface{} `json:"args"`
    38→	Status string                 `json:"status,omitempty"`
    39→}
    40→
    41→// ParseTranscript parses raw JSON content into a transcript structure
    42→func ParseTranscript(data []byte) (*GeminiTranscript, error) {
    43→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
    44→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &transcript); err != nil {
    45→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    46→	}
    47→	return &transcript, nil
    48→}
    49→
    50→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript data
    51→func ExtractModifiedFiles(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
    52→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return nil, err
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	return ExtractModifiedFilesFromTranscript(transcript), nil
    58→}
    59→
    60→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromTranscript extracts files from a parsed transcript
    61→func ExtractModifiedFilesFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) []string {
    62→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
    63→	var files []string
    64→
    65→	for _, msg := range transcript.Messages {
    66→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
    67→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
    68→			continue
    69→		}
    70→
    71→		// Process tool calls in this message
    72→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
    73→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
    74→			isModifyTool := false
    75→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
    76→				if toolCall.Name == name {
    77→					isModifyTool = true
    78→					break
    79→				}
    80→			}
    81→
    82→			if !isModifyTool {
    83→				continue
    84→			}
    85→
    86→			// Extract file path from args map
    87→			var file string
    88→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
    89→				file = fp
    90→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
    91→				file = p
    92→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
    93→				file = fn
    94→			}
    95→
    96→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
    97→				fileSet[file] = true
    98→				files = append(files, file)
    99→			}
   100→		}
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return files
   104→}
   105→
   106→// ExtractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from transcript data
   107→func ExtractLastUserPrompt(data []byte) (string, error) {
   108→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		return "", err
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	return ExtractLastUserPromptFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   114→}
   115→
   116→// ExtractLastUserPromptFromTranscript extracts the last user prompt from a parsed transcript
   117→func ExtractLastUserPromptFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) string {
   118→	for i := len(transcript.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   119→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   120→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeUser {
   121→			continue
   122→		}
   123→
   124→		// Content is now a string field
   125→		if msg.Content != "" {
   126→			return msg.Content
   127→		}
   128→	}
   129→	return ""
   130→}
   131→
   132→// ExtractAllUserPrompts extracts all user messages from transcript data
   133→func ExtractAllUserPrompts(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   134→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   135→	if err != nil {
   136→		return nil, err
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	return ExtractAllUserPromptsFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   140→}
   141→
   142→// ExtractAllUserPromptsFromTranscript extracts all user prompts from a parsed transcript
   143→func ExtractAllUserPromptsFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) []string {
   144→	var prompts []string
   145→	for _, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   146→		if msg.Type == MessageTypeUser && msg.Content != "" {
   147→			prompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)
   148→		}
   149→	}
   150→	return prompts
   151→}
   152→
   153→// ExtractLastAssistantMessage extracts the last gemini response from transcript data
   154→func ExtractLastAssistantMessage(data []byte) (string, error) {
   155→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   156→	if err != nil {
   157→		return "", err
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	return ExtractLastAssistantMessageFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   161→}
   162→
   163→// ExtractLastAssistantMessageFromTranscript extracts the last gemini response from a parsed transcript
   164→func ExtractLastAssistantMessageFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) string {
   165→	for i := len(transcript.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   166→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   167→		if msg.Type == MessageTypeGemini && msg.Content != "" {
   168→			return msg.Content
   169→		}
   170→	}
   171→	return ""
   172→}
   173→
   174→// GetLastMessageID returns the ID of the last message in the transcript.
   175→// Returns empty string if the transcript is empty or the last message has no ID.
   176→func GetLastMessageID(data []byte) (string, error) {
   177→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		return "", err
   180→	}
   181→	return GetLastMessageIDFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   182→}
   183→
   184→// GetLastMessageIDFromTranscript returns the ID of the last message in a parsed transcript.
   185→// Returns empty string if the transcript is empty or the last message has no ID.
   186→func GetLastMessageIDFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) string {
   187→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   188→		return ""
   189→	}
   190→	return transcript.Messages[len(transcript.Messages)-1].ID
   191→}
   192→
   193→// GetLastMessageIDFromFile reads a transcript file and returns the last message's ID.
   194→// Returns empty string if the file doesn't exist, is empty, or has no messages with IDs.
   195→func GetLastMessageIDFromFile(path string) (string, error) {
   196→	if path == "" {
   197→		return "", nil
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   201→	if err != nil {
   202→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   203→			return "", nil
   204→		}
   205→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	if len(data) == 0 {
   209→		return "", nil
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	return GetLastMessageID(data)
   213→}
   214→
   215→// SliceFromMessage returns a Gemini transcript scoped to messages starting from
   216→// startMessageIndex. This is the Gemini equivalent of transcript.SliceFromLine —
   217→// for Gemini's single JSON blob, scoping is done by message index rather than line offset.
   218→// Returns the original data if startMessageIndex <= 0.
   219→// Returns nil if startMessageIndex exceeds the number of messages.
   220→func SliceFromMessage(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) []byte {
   221→	if len(data) == 0 || startMessageIndex <= 0 {
   222→		return data
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	t, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		return nil
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	if startMessageIndex >= len(t.Messages) {
   231→		return nil
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	scoped := &GeminiTranscript{
   235→		Messages: t.Messages[startMessageIndex:],
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	out, err := json.Marshal(scoped)
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		return nil
   241→	}
   242→	return out
   243→}
   244→
   245→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Gemini transcript.
   246→// This is specific to Gemini's API format where each message may have a tokens object
   247→// with input, output, cached, thoughts, tool, and total counts.
   248→// Only processes messages from startMessageIndex onwards (0-indexed).
   249→func CalculateTokenUsage(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   250→	var transcript struct {
   251→		Messages []geminiMessageWithTokens `json:"messages"`
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &transcript); err != nil {
   255→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   259→
   260→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   261→		// Skip messages before startMessageIndex
   262→		if i < startMessageIndex {
   263→			continue
   264→		}
   265→
   266→		// Only count tokens from gemini (assistant) messages
   267→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   268→			continue
   269→		}
   270→
   271→		if msg.Tokens == nil {
   272→			continue
   273→		}
   274→
   275→		usage.APICallCount++
   276→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Tokens.Input
   277→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Tokens.Output
   278→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Tokens.Cached
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	return usage
   282→}
   283→
   284→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a Gemini transcript file.
   285→// If startMessageIndex > 0, only considers messages from that index onwards.
   286→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startMessageIndex int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   287→	if path == "" {
   288→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   294→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   295→		}
   296→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	return CalculateTokenUsage(data, startMessageIndex), nil
   300→}
   301→
```

```
   234→func TestGetLastMessageID(t *testing.T) {
   235→	t.Parallel()
   236→
   237→	tests := []struct {
   238→		name    string
   239→		data    string
   240→		want    string
   241→		wantErr bool
   242→	}{
   243→		{
   244→			name: "transcript with message IDs",
   245→			data: `{"messages": [
   246→				{"id": "msg-1", "type": "user", "content": "hello"},
   247→				{"id": "msg-2", "type": "gemini", "content": "hi there"}
   248→			]}`,
   249→			want:    "msg-2",
   250→			wantErr: false,
   251→		},
   252→		{
   253→			name:    "empty transcript",
   254→			data:    `{"messages": []}`,
   255→			want:    "",
   256→			wantErr: false,
   257→		},
   258→		{
   259→			name: "message without ID (empty ID field)",
   260→			data: `{"messages": [
   261→				{"type": "user", "content": "hello"},
   262→				{"type": "gemini", "content": "hi"}
   263→			]}`,
   264→			want:    "",
   265→			wantErr: false,
   266→		},
   267→		{
   268→			name: "mixed - some with IDs, some without",
   269→			data: `{"messages": [
   270→				{"id": "msg-1", "type": "user", "content": "hello"},
   271→				{"type": "gemini", "content": "hi"}
   272→			]}`,
   273→			want:    "",
   274→			wantErr: false,
   275→		},
   276→		{
   277→			name:    "invalid JSON",
   278→			data:    `not valid json`,
   279→			want:    "",
   280→			wantErr: true,
   281→		},
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	for _, tt := range tests {
   285→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   286→			t.Parallel()
   287→			got, err := GetLastMessageID([]byte(tt.data))
   288→			if (err != nil) != tt.wantErr {
   289→				t.Errorf("GetLastMessageID() error = %v, wantErr %v", err, tt.wantErr)
   290→				return
   291→			}
   292→			if got != tt.want {
   293→				t.Errorf("GetLastMessageID() = %q, want %q", got, tt.want)
   294→			}
   295→		})
   296→	}
   297→}

   460→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_BasicMessages(t *testing.T) {
   461→	t.Parallel()
   462→
   463→	// Gemini transcript with token usage in messages
   464→	data := []byte(`{
   465→  "messages": [
   466→    {"id": "1", "type": "user", "content": "hello"},
   467→    {"id": "2", "type": "gemini", "content": "hi there", "tokens": {"input": 10, "output": 20, "cached": 5, "thoughts": 0, "tool": 0, "total": 35}},
   468→    {"id": "3", "type": "user", "content": "how are you?"},
   469→    {"id": "4", "type": "gemini", "content": "I'm doing well", "tokens": {"input": 15, "output": 25, "cached": 3, "thoughts": 0, "tool": 0, "total": 43}}
   470→  ]
   471→}`)
   472→
   473→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(data, 0)
   474→
   475→	// Should have 2 API calls (2 gemini messages)
   476→	if usage.APICallCount != 2 {
   477→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 2", usage.APICallCount)
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	// Input tokens: 10 + 15 = 25
   481→	if usage.InputTokens != 25 {
   482→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 25", usage.InputTokens)
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	// Output tokens: 20 + 25 = 45
   486→	if usage.OutputTokens != 45 {
   487→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 45", usage.OutputTokens)
   488→	}
   489→
   490→	// Cache read tokens: 5 + 3 = 8
   491→	if usage.CacheReadTokens != 8 {
   492→		t.Errorf("CacheReadTokens = %d, want 8", usage.CacheReadTokens)
   493→	}
   494→}
   495→
   496→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_StartIndex(t *testing.T) {
   497→	t.Parallel()
   498→
   499→	// Gemini transcript with 4 messages - test starting from index 2
   500→	data := []byte(`{
   501→  "messages": [
   502→    {"id": "1", "type": "user", "content": "hello"},
   503→    {"id": "2", "type": "gemini", "content": "hi", "tokens": {"input": 10, "output": 20, "cached": 0, "total": 30}},
   504→    {"id": "3", "type": "user", "content": "how are you?"},
   505→    {"id": "4", "type": "gemini", "content": "great", "tokens": {"input": 15, "output": 25, "cached": 5, "total": 45}}
   506→  ]
   507→}`)
   508→
   509→	// Start from index 2 - should only count the last gemini message
   510→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(data, 2)
   511→
   512→	// Should have 1 API call (only the gemini message at index 3)
   513→	if usage.APICallCount != 1 {
   514→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 1", usage.APICallCount)
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Only tokens from message at index 3
   518→	if usage.InputTokens != 15 {
   519→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 15", usage.InputTokens)
   520→	}
   521→
   522→	if usage.OutputTokens != 25 {
   523→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 25", usage.OutputTokens)
   524→	}
   525→
   526→	if usage.CacheReadTokens != 5 {
   527→		t.Errorf("CacheReadTokens = %d, want 5", usage.CacheReadTokens)
   528→	}
   529→}
   530→
   531→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_IgnoresUserMessages(t *testing.T) {
   532→	t.Parallel()
   533→
   534→	// Even if user messages have tokens (they shouldn't), they should be ignored
   535→	data := []byte(`{
   536→  "messages": [
   537→    {"id": "1", "type": "user", "content": "hello", "tokens": {"input": 100, "output": 100, "cached": 100, "total": 300}},
   538→    {"id": "2", "type": "gemini", "content": "hi", "tokens": {"input": 10, "output": 20, "cached": 5, "total": 35}}
   539→  ]
   540→}`)
   541→
   542→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(data, 0)
   543→
   544→	// Should only count gemini message tokens
   545→	if usage.APICallCount != 1 {
   546→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 1", usage.APICallCount)
   547→	}
   548→
   549→	if usage.InputTokens != 10 {
   550→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 10", usage.InputTokens)
   551→	}
   552→
   553→	if usage.OutputTokens != 20 {
   554→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 20", usage.OutputTokens)
   555→	}
   556→}
   557→
   558→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_EmptyTranscript(t *testing.T) {
   559→	t.Parallel()
   560→
   561→	data := []byte(`{"messages": []}`)

   578→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_InvalidJSON(t *testing.T) {
```

```
   133→		"reason": "exit"
   134→	}`
   135→
   136→	hookInput, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, bytes.NewReader([]byte(input)))
   137→	if err != nil {
   138→		t.Fatalf("ParseHookInput() error = %v", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	if hookInput.SessionID != testSessionID {
   142→		t.Errorf("SessionID = %q, want %s", hookInput.SessionID, testSessionID)
   143→	}
   144→	if hookInput.RawData["reason"] != "exit" {
   145→		t.Errorf("reason = %v, want exit", hookInput.RawData["reason"])
   146→	}
   147→}

   228→func TestResolveSessionFile(t *testing.T) {
   229→	t.Parallel()
   230→
   231→	t.Run("finds existing Gemini-named file", func(t *testing.T) {
   232→		t.Parallel()
   233→		tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   234→		ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   235→
   236→		// Create a file with Gemini's naming convention
   237→		geminiFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "session-2026-02-10T09-19-0544a0f5.json")
   238→		if err := os.WriteFile(geminiFile, []byte("{}"), 0o644); err != nil {
   239→			t.Fatal(err)
   240→		}
   241→
   242→		result := ag.ResolveSessionFile(tmpDir, "0544a0f5-46a6-41b3-a89c-e7804df731b8")
   243→		if result != geminiFile {
   244→			t.Errorf("ResolveSessionFile() = %q, want %q", result, geminiFile)
   245→		}
   246→	})
   247→
   248→	t.Run("falls back to default when no match", func(t *testing.T) {
   249→		t.Parallel()
   250→		tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   251→		ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   252→
   253→		result := ag.ResolveSessionFile(tmpDir, "0544a0f5-46a6-41b3-a89c-e7804df731b8")
   254→		expected := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "0544a0f5-46a6-41b3-a89c-e7804df731b8.json")
   255→		if result != expected {
   256→			t.Errorf("ResolveSessionFile() = %q, want %q", result, expected)
   257→		}
   258→	})
   259→
   260→	t.Run("handles short session ID", func(t *testing.T) {
   261→		t.Parallel()
   262→		tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   263→		ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   264→
   265→		// Short ID (less than 8 chars) should use entire ID as prefix
   266→		result := ag.ResolveSessionFile(tmpDir, "abc123")
   267→		expected := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "abc123.json")
   268→		if result != expected {
   269→			t.Errorf("ResolveSessionFile() = %q, want %q", result, expected)
   270→		}
   271→	})
   272→}

   325→func TestReadSession(t *testing.T) {
   326→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   327→
   328→	// Create a transcript file
   329→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "transcript.json")
   330→	transcriptContent := `{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hello"}]}`
   331→	if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(transcriptContent), 0o644); err != nil {
   332→		t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   336→	input := &agent.HookInput{
   337→		SessionID:  "test-session",
   338→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   339→	}
   340→
   341→	session, err := ag.ReadSession(input)
   342→	if err != nil {
   343→		t.Fatalf("ReadSession() error = %v", err)

   346→	if session.SessionID != "test-session" {
   347→		t.Errorf("SessionID = %q, want test-session", session.SessionID)
   348→	}
   349→	if session.AgentName != agent.AgentNameGemini {
   350→		t.Errorf("AgentName = %q, want %q", session.AgentName, agent.AgentNameGemini)
   351→	}
   352→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   353→		t.Error("NativeData is empty")
   354→	}
   355→}

   462→func TestGetSupportedHooks(t *testing.T) {
   463→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   464→	hooks := ag.GetSupportedHooks()
   465→
   466→	expected := []agent.HookType{
   467→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   468→		agent.HookSessionEnd,       // Maps to Gemini's SessionEnd (explicit exit/logout)
   469→		agent.HookStop,             // Maps to Gemini's AfterAgent (end of response)
   470→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, // Maps to Gemini's BeforeAgent
   471→		agent.HookPreToolUse,       // Maps to Gemini's BeforeTool
   472→		agent.HookPostToolUse,      // Maps to Gemini's AfterTool
   473→	}
   474→
   475→	if len(hooks) != len(expected) {
   476→		t.Errorf("GetSupportedHooks() returned %d hooks, want %d", len(hooks), len(expected))
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	for i, hook := range expected {
   480→		if hooks[i] != hook {
   481→			t.Errorf("GetSupportedHooks()[%d] = %v, want %v", i, hooks[i], hook)
   482→		}
   483→	}
   484→}

   486→// Chunking tests
   487→
   488→func TestChunkTranscript_SmallContent(t *testing.T) {
   489→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   490→
   491→	content := []byte(`{"messages":[{"type":"user","content":"hello"},{"type":"gemini","content":"hi there"}]}`)
   492→
   493→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, agent.MaxChunkSize)
   494→	if err != nil {
   495→		t.Fatalf("ChunkTranscript() error = %v", err)
   496→	}
   497→	if len(chunks) != 1 {
   498→		t.Errorf("Expected 1 chunk, got %d", len(chunks))
   499→	}
   500→}
   501→
   502→func TestChunkTranscript_LargeContent(t *testing.T) {
   503→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   504→
   505→	// Create a transcript with many messages that exceeds maxSize
   506→	var messages []GeminiMessage
   507→	for i := range 100 {
   508→		messages = append(messages, GeminiMessage{
   509→			Type:    "user",
   510→			Content: fmt.Sprintf("message %d with some content to make it larger: %s", i, strings.Repeat("x", 500)),
   511→		})
   512→	}
   513→
   514→	transcript := GeminiTranscript{Messages: messages}
   515→	content, err := json.Marshal(transcript)
   516→	if err != nil {
   517→		t.Fatalf("Failed to marshal test transcript: %v", err)
   518→	}
   519→
   520→	// Use a small maxSize to force chunking
   521→	maxSize := 5000
   522→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, maxSize)
   523→	if err != nil {
   524→		t.Fatalf("ChunkTranscript() error = %v", err)
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	if len(chunks) < 2 {
   528→		t.Errorf("Expected at least 2 chunks for large content, got %d", len(chunks))
   529→	}
   530→
   531→	// Verify each chunk is valid JSON with messages array
   532→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   533→		var parsed GeminiTranscript
   534→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &parsed); err != nil {
   535→			t.Errorf("Chunk %d is not valid Gemini JSON: %v", i, err)
   536→		}
   537→		if len(parsed.Messages) == 0 {
   538→			t.Errorf("Chunk %d has no messages", i)
   539→		}
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Verify reassembly gives back all messages
   543→	reassembled, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
   544→	if err != nil {
   545→		t.Fatalf("ReassembleTranscript() error = %v", err)
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	var result GeminiTranscript
   549→	if err := json.Unmarshal(reassembled, &result); err != nil {
   550→		t.Fatalf("Failed to unmarshal reassembled content: %v", err)
   551→	}
   552→
   553→	if len(result.Messages) != len(messages) {
   554→		t.Errorf("Reassembled message count = %d, want %d", len(result.Messages), len(messages))
   555→	}
   556→}
   557→
   558→func TestChunkTranscript_EmptyMessages(t *testing.T) {
   559→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   560→
   561→	content := []byte(`{"messages":[]}`)

   575→func REDACTED(t *testing.T) {

   592→func TestChunkTranscript_RoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
   593→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   594→
   595→	// Create a realistic transcript
   596→	original := GeminiTranscript{
   597→		Messages: []GeminiMessage{
   598→			{Type: "user", Content: "Write a hello world program"},
   599→			{Type: "gemini", Content: "Sure, here's a hello world program:", ToolCalls: []GeminiToolCall{
   600→				{ID: "1", Name: "write_file", Args: map[string]interface{}{"path": "main.go", "content": "package main\n\nfunc main() {\n\tprintln(\"Hello, World!\")\n}"}},
   601→			}},
   602→			{Type: "user", Content: "Now add a function"},
   603→			{Type: "gemini", Content: "I'll add a greet function:", ToolCalls: []GeminiToolCall{
   604→				{ID: "2", Name: "edit_file", Args: map[string]interface{}{"path": "main.go"}},
   605→			}},
   606→		},
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	content, err := json.Marshal(original)
   610→	if err != nil {
   611→		t.Fatalf("Failed to marshal original: %v", err)
   612→	}
   613→
   614→	// Use small maxSize to force chunking
   615→	maxSize := 200
   616→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, maxSize)
   617→	if err != nil {
   618→		t.Fatalf("ChunkTranscript() error = %v", err)
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	reassembled, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
   622→	if err != nil {
   623→		t.Fatalf("ReassembleTranscript() error = %v", err)
   624→	}
   625→
   626→	var result GeminiTranscript
   627→	if err := json.Unmarshal(reassembled, &result); err != nil {
   628→		t.Fatalf("Failed to unmarshal reassembled: %v", err)
   629→	}
   630→
   631→	if len(result.Messages) != len(original.Messages) {
   632→		t.Fatalf("Message count mismatch: got %d, want %d", len(result.Messages), len(original.Messages))
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	for i, msg := range result.Messages {
   636→		if msg.Type != original.Messages[i].Type {
   637→			t.Errorf("Message %d type = %q, want %q", i, msg.Type, original.Messages[i].Type)
   638→		}
   639→		if msg.Content != original.Messages[i].Content {
   640→			t.Errorf("Message %d content mismatch", i)
   641→		}
   642→		if len(msg.ToolCalls) != len(original.Messages[i].ToolCalls) {
   643→			t.Errorf("Message %d toolCalls count = %d, want %d", i, len(msg.ToolCalls), len(original.Messages[i].ToolCalls))
   644→		}
   645→	}
   646→}
   647→
   648→func TestReassembleTranscript_SingleChunk(t *testing.T) {
   649→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   650→
   651→	content := []byte(`{"messages":[{"type":"user","content":"hello"}]}`)
   652→	chunks := [][]byte{content}
   653→
   654→	result, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
   655→	if err != nil {
   656→		t.Fatalf("ReassembleTranscript() error = %v", err)
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	var parsed GeminiTranscript
   660→	if err := json.Unmarshal(result, &parsed); err != nil {
   661→		t.Fatalf("Failed to unmarshal result: %v", err)
   662→	}
   663→
   664→	if len(parsed.Messages) != 1 {
   665→		t.Errorf("Expected 1 message, got %d", len(parsed.Messages))
   666→	}
   667→}
   668→
   669→func TestReassembleTranscript_MultipleChunks(t *testing.T) {
   670→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   671→
   672→	chunk1 := []byte(`{"messages":[{"type":"user","content":"hello"}]}`)
   673→	chunk2 := []byte(`{"messages":[{"type":"gemini","content":"hi"}]}`)
   674→	chunks := [][]byte{chunk1, chunk2}
   675→
   676→	result, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
   677→	if err != nil {
   678→		t.Fatalf("ReassembleTranscript() error = %v", err)
   679→	}
   680→
   681→	var parsed GeminiTranscript
   682→	if err := json.Unmarshal(result, &parsed); err != nil {
   683→		t.Fatalf("Failed to unmarshal result: %v", err)
   684→	}
   685→
   686→	if len(parsed.Messages) != 2 {
   687→		t.Errorf("Expected 2 messages, got %d", len(parsed.Messages))
   688→	}
   689→	if parsed.Messages[0].Content != "hello" {
   690→		t.Errorf("First message content = %q, want hello", parsed.Messages[0].Content)
   691→	}
   692→	if parsed.Messages[1].Content != "hi" {
   693→		t.Errorf("Second message content = %q, want hi", parsed.Messages[1].Content)
   694→	}
   695→}
   696→
   697→func TestReassembleTranscript_InvalidChunk(t *testing.T) {
   698→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   699→
   700→	chunk1 := []byte(`{"messages":[{"type":"user","content":"hello"}]}`)
   701→	chunk2 := []byte(`not valid json`)
   702→	chunks := [][]byte{chunk1, chunk2}
   703→
   704→	_, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
   705→	if err == nil {
   706→		t.Error("ReassembleTranscript() should error on invalid JSON chunk")
   707→	}
   708→}
   709→
   710→func TestReassembleTranscript_EmptyChunks(t *testing.T) {
   711→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   712→
   713→	result, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript([][]byte{})
   714→	if err != nil {
   715→		t.Fatalf("ReassembleTranscript() error = %v", err)
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	// Should return valid JSON with empty messages array
   719→	var parsed GeminiTranscript
   720→	if err := json.Unmarshal(result, &parsed); err != nil {
   721→		t.Fatalf("Failed to unmarshal result: %v", err)
   722→	}
   723→
   724→	if len(parsed.Messages) != 0 {
   725→		t.Errorf("Expected 0 messages for empty chunks, got %d", len(parsed.Messages))
   726→	}
   727→}
   728→
   729→func TestChunkTranscript_SingleOversizedMessage(t *testing.T) {
   730→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   731→
   732→	// Create a single message that exceeds maxSize
   733→	largeContent := strings.Repeat("x", 1000)
   734→	transcript := GeminiTranscript{
   735→		Messages: []GeminiMessage{
   736→			{Type: "user", Content: largeContent},
   737→		},
   738→	}
   739→
   740→	content, err := json.Marshal(transcript)
   741→	if err != nil {
   742→		t.Fatalf("Failed to marshal: %v", err)
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	// maxSize smaller than the single message
   746→	maxSize := 100
   747→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, maxSize)
   748→	if err != nil {
   749→		t.Fatalf("ChunkTranscript() error = %v", err)
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	// Should still produce a chunk (can't split a single message)
   753→	if len(chunks) != 1 {
   754→		t.Errorf("Expected 1 chunk for single oversized message, got %d", len(chunks))
   755→	}
   756→
   757→	// Verify it's valid and contains the message
   758→	var parsed GeminiTranscript
   759→	if err := json.Unmarshal(chunks[0], &parsed); err != nil {
   760→		t.Fatalf("Chunk is not valid JSON: %v", err)
   761→	}
   762→	if len(parsed.Messages) != 1 {
   763→		t.Errorf("Expected 1 message in chunk, got %d", len(parsed.Messages))
   764→	}
   765→}
   766→
   767→func TestChunkTranscript_ChunkBoundary(t *testing.T) {
   768→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   769→
   770→	// Create messages where the boundary matters
   771→	messages := []GeminiMessage{
   772→		{Type: "user", Content: "msg1"},
   773→		{Type: "gemini", Content: "msg2"},
   774→		{Type: "user", Content: "msg3"},
   775→		{Type: "gemini", Content: "msg4"},
   776→	}
   777→
   778→	transcript := GeminiTranscript{Messages: messages}
   779→	content, err := json.Marshal(transcript)
   780→	if err != nil {
   781→		t.Fatalf("Failed to marshal: %v", err)
   782→	}
   783→
   784→	// Calculate size to get exactly 2 chunks with 2 messages each
   785→	// The base structure is {"messages":[]} = 15 chars
   786→	// Each message is roughly 25-30 chars including comma
   787→	maxSize := 100
   788→
   789→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, maxSize)
   790→	if err != nil {
   791→		t.Fatalf("ChunkTranscript() error = %v", err)
   792→	}
   793→
   794→	// Verify all messages are preserved across chunks
   795→	totalMessages := 0
   796→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   797→		var parsed GeminiTranscript
   798→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &parsed); err != nil {
   799→			t.Fatalf("Chunk is not valid JSON: %v", err)
   800→		}
   801→		totalMessages += len(parsed.Messages)
   802→	}
   803→
   804→	if totalMessages != len(messages) {
   805→		t.Errorf("Total messages across chunks = %d, want %d", totalMessages, len(messages))
   806→	}
   807→}
   808→
   809→func TestChunkTranscript_PreservesMessageOrder(t *testing.T) {
   810→	ag := &GeminiCLIAgent{}
   811→
   812→	// Create messages with numbered content to verify order
   813→	var messages []GeminiMessage
   814→	for i := range 20 {
   815→		messages = append(messages, GeminiMessage{
   816→			Type:    "user",
   817→			Content: fmt.Sprintf("message-%03d", i),
   818→		})
   819→	}
   820→
   821→	transcript := GeminiTranscript{Messages: messages}
   822→	content, err := json.Marshal(transcript)
   823→	if err != nil {
   824→		t.Fatalf("Failed to marshal: %v", err)
   825→	}
   826→
   827→	// Small maxSize to force multiple chunks
   828→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, 200)
   829→	if err != nil {
   830→		t.Fatalf("ChunkTranscript() error = %v", err)
   831→	}
   832→
   833→	reassembled, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
   834→	if err != nil {
   835→		t.Fatalf("ReassembleTranscript() error = %v", err)
   836→	}
   837→
   838→	var result GeminiTranscript
   839→	if err := json.Unmarshal(reassembled, &result); err != nil {
   840→		t.Fatalf("Failed to unmarshal: %v", err)
   841→	}
   842→
   843→	// Verify message order is preserved
   844→	for i, msg := range result.Messages {
   845→		expected := fmt.Sprintf("message-%03d", i)
   846→		if msg.Content != expected {
   847→			t.Errorf("Message %d content = %q, want %q", i, msg.Content, expected)
   848→		}
   849→	}
   850→}
```

```
    26→// GeminiCLIAgent implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.

    31→func NewGeminiCLIAgent() agent.Agent {

    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.

    48→}
    49→
    50→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    51→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .gemini directory
    61→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Gemini's hook config file.
    74→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".gemini/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Gemini CLI supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Gemini CLI hook input from stdin.
    84→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   103→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		// Store Gemini-specific fields in RawData
   110→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   111→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   112→		if raw.Source != "" {
   113→			input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   114→		}
   115→		if raw.Reason != "" {
   116→			input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   117→		}
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   120→		// BeforeAgent is Gemini's equivalent to Claude's UserPromptSubmit
   121→		// It provides the user's prompt in the "prompt" field
   122→		var raw agentHookInputRaw
   123→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   124→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse agent hook input: %w", err)
   125→		}
   126→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   127→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   128→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   129→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   130→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   131→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   132→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   133→		}
   134→
   135→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   136→		var raw toolHookInputRaw
   137→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   138→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   139→		}
   140→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   141→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   142→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   143→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   144→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   145→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResponse
   146→		}
   147→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   148→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	return input, nil
   152→}
   153→
   154→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   155→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   156→	return input.SessionID
   157→}
   158→
   159→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Gemini uses for config/state.
   160→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".gemini"} }
   161→
   162→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
   163→// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
   164→// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
   165→// back to <dir>/<id>.json if no match is found.
   166→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   167→	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
   168→	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
   169→	shortID := agentSessionID
   170→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   171→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
   175→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
   176→	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
   177→		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
   178→		return matches[len(matches)-1]
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Fallback: construct a default path
   182→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".json")
   183→}
   184→
   185→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   186→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   187→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   188→	// Check for test environment override
   189→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   190→		return override, nil
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Gemini uses a hash of the project path for the directory name
   199→	projectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)
   200→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   204→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   205→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   206→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   207→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Read the raw JSON file
   211→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   217→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   220→		modifiedFiles = nil
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   224→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   225→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   226→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   227→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   228→		NativeData:    data,
   229→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   230→	}, nil
   231→}
   232→
   233→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   234→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   235→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   236→	if session == nil {
   237→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   241→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   246→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   250→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Write the raw JSON data
   254→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	return nil
   259→}
   260→
   261→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   262→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   263→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   264→}
   265→
   266→// SanitizePathForGemini converts a path to Gemini's project directory format.
   267→// Gemini uses a hash-like sanitization similar to Claude.
   268→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   269→
   270→func SanitizePathForGemini(path string) string {
   271→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   272→}
   273→
   274→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   275→
   276→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   277→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   278→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   279→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   280→	if path == "" {
   281→		return 0, nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   287→			return 0, nil
   288→		}
   289→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	if len(data) == 0 {
   293→		return 0, nil
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   302→}
   303→
   304→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   305→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   306→// Returns:
   307→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   308→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   309→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   310→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   311→	if path == "" {
   312→		return nil, 0, nil
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   316→	if readErr != nil {
   317→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   318→			return nil, 0, nil
   319→		}
   320→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	if len(data) == 0 {
   324→		return nil, 0, nil
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   328→	if parseErr != nil {
   329→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   333→
   334→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   335→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   336→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   337→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   338→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   339→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   340→			continue
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Process tool calls in this message
   344→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   345→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   346→			isModifyTool := false
   347→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   348→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   349→					isModifyTool = true
   350→					break
   351→				}
   352→			}
   353→
   354→			if !isModifyTool {
   355→				continue
   356→			}
   357→
   358→			// Extract file path from args map
   359→			var file string
   360→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   361→				file = fp
   362→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   363→				file = p
   364→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   365→				file = fn
   366→			}
   367→
   368→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   369→				fileSet[file] = true
   370→				files = append(files, file)
   371→			}
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   376→}
   377→
   378→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   379→
   380→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   381→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   382→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   383→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   384→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   385→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   386→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   387→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   388→		if chunkErr != nil {
   389→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   390→		}
   391→		return chunks, nil
   392→	}
   393→
   394→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   395→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	var chunks [][]byte
   399→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   400→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   401→
   402→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   403→		// Marshal message to get its size
   404→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   405→		if err != nil {
   406→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   407→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   408→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   409→			)
   410→			continue
   411→		}
   412→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   413→
   414→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   415→			// Save current chunk
   416→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   417→			if err != nil {
   418→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   419→			}
   420→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   421→
   422→			// Start new chunk
   423→			currentMessages = nil
   424→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   425→		}
   426→
   427→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   428→		currentSize += msgSize
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	// Add the last chunk
   432→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   433→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   434→		if err != nil {
   435→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   436→		}
   437→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   441→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   442→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	return chunks, nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   449→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   450→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   451→
   452→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   453→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   454→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   455→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   456→		}
   457→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   461→	if err != nil {
   462→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   463→	}
   464→	return result, nil
   465→}
   466→
```

```
   425→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   426→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   427→// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   428→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   429→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   430→	if content == "" {
   431→		return 0
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   435→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   436→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   437→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   438→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   439→		}
   440→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   441→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   442→			return 0
   443→		}
   444→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   445→	}
   446→
   447→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   448→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   449→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
```

```
   831→
   832→// sessionHasNewContent checks if a session has new transcript content
   833→// beyond what was already condensed.
   834→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   835→	// Get shadow branch
   836→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   837→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   838→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   839→	if err != nil {
   840→		// No shadow branch means no Stop has happened since the last condensation.
   841→		// However, the agent may have done work (including commits) without a Stop.
   842→		// Check the live transcript to detect this scenario.
   843→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   844→	}
   845→
   846→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   847→	if err != nil {
   848→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   849→	}
   850→
   851→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   852→	if err != nil {
   853→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   854→	}
   855→
   856→	// Look for transcript file
   857→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + state.SessionID
   858→	var transcriptLines int
   859→	var hasTranscriptFile bool
   860→
   861→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   862→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   863→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   864→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   865→		}
   866→	} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   867→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   868→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   869→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   870→		}
   871→	}
   872→
   873→	// If shadow branch exists but has no transcript (e.g., carry-forward from mid-session commit),
   874→	// check if the session has FilesTouched. Carry-forward sets FilesTouched with remaining files.
   875→	if !hasTranscriptFile {
   876→		if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   877→			// Shadow branch has files from carry-forward - check if staged files overlap
   878→			// AND have matching content (content-aware check).
   879→			stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
   880→			result := stagedFilesOverlapWithContent(repo, tree, stagedFiles, state.FilesTouched)
   881→			return result, nil
   882→		}
   883→		// No transcript and no FilesTouched - fall back to live transcript check
   884→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   885→	}
   886→
   887→	// Has new content if there are more lines than already condensed
   888→	return transcriptLines > state.CheckpointTranscriptStart, nil
   889→}
   890→
   891→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
   892→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
   893→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
   894→//
   895→// Returns true if:
   896→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
   897→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
   898→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
   899→//
   900→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
   901→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
   902→//
   903→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
   904→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   905→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   906→
   907→	modifiedFiles, ok := s.extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
   908→	if !ok || len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
   909→		return false, nil
   910→	}
   911→
   912→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: found file modifications",
   913→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   914→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
   915→	)
   916→
   917→	// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files
   918→	// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include
   919→	// files the agent actually modified
   920→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
   921→
   922→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: comparing staged vs modified",
   923→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   924→		slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
   925→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
   926→	)
   927→
   928→	if !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {
   929→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no overlap between staged and modified files",
   930→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   931→		)
   932→		return false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work
   933→	}
   934→
   935→	return true, nil
   936→}
   937→
   938→// extractFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified file paths from the live transcript.
   939→// Returns empty slice if extraction fails (fail-open behavior for hooks).
   940→// Extracts ALL files from the transcript (offset 0) because this is used for carry-forward
   941→// computation which needs to know all files touched, not just new ones.
   942→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) []string {
   943→	return s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, 0)
   944→}
   945→
   946→// extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified files from the live
   947→// transcript that are NEW since the last condensation. Returns the normalized file list
   948→// and whether the extraction succeeded. Used by sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript
   949→// to detect agent work.
```

```
     1→package geminicli

     3→import "encoding/json"
     4→
     5→// GeminiSettings represents the .gemini/settings.json structure
     6→type GeminiSettings struct {
     7→	HooksConfig GeminiHooksConfig `json:"hooksConfig,omitempty"`
     8→	Hooks       GeminiHooks       `json:"hooks,omitempty"`
     9→}
    10→
    11→// GeminiHooksConfig contains tool-related settings
    12→type GeminiHooksConfig struct {
    13→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled,omitempty"`
    14→}
    15→
    16→// GeminiHooks contains all hook configurations
    17→type GeminiHooks struct {
    18→	// Hooks are only executed when hooksConfig.enabled is true in .gemini/settings.json.
    19→	SessionStart        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    20→	SessionEnd          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    21→	BeforeAgent         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeAgent,omitempty"`
    22→	AfterAgent          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterAgent,omitempty"`
    23→	BeforeModel         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeModel,omitempty"`
    24→	AfterModel          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterModel,omitempty"`
    25→	BeforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeToolSelection,omitempty"`
    26→	BeforeTool          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeTool,omitempty"`
    27→	AfterTool           []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterTool,omitempty"`
    28→	PreCompress         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"PreCompress,omitempty"`
    29→	Notification        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"Notification,omitempty"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// GeminiHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns
    33→type GeminiHookMatcher struct {
    34→	Matcher string            `json:"matcher,omitempty"`
    35→	Hooks   []GeminiHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→// GeminiHookEntry represents a single hook command.
    39→// Unlike Claude Code, Gemini CLI requires a "name" field for each hook entry.
    40→type GeminiHookEntry struct {
    41→	Name    string `json:"name"`
    42→	Type    string `json:"type"`
    43→	Command string `json:"command"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd hooks
    47→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    48→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    49→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    50→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    51→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    52→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    53→	Source         string `json:"source,omitempty"` // For SessionStart: startup, resume, clear
    54→	Reason         string `json:"reason,omitempty"` // For SessionEnd: exit, logout
    55→}
    56→
    57→// agentHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeAgent/AfterAgent hooks.
    58→// BeforeAgent includes the user's prompt, similar to Claude's UserPromptSubmit.
    59→type agentHookInputRaw struct {
    60→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    61→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    62→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    63→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    64→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    65→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt,omitempty"` // User's prompt (BeforeAgent only)
    66→}
    67→
    68→// toolHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeTool/AfterTool hooks
    69→type toolHookInputRaw struct {
    70→	SessionID      string          `json:"session_id"`
    71→	TranscriptPath string          `json:"transcript_path"`
    72→	Cwd            string          `json:"cwd"`
    73→	HookEventName  string          `json:"hook_event_name"`
    74→	Timestamp      string          `json:"timestamp"`
    75→	ToolName       string          `json:"tool_name"`
    76→	ToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:"tool_input"`
    77→	ToolResponse   json.RawMessage `json:"tool_response,omitempty"` // Only for AfterTool
    78→}
    79→
    80→// Tool names used in Gemini CLI that modify files
    81→// Note: Gemini CLI uses different names in different contexts:
    82→// - Internal/transcript names: write_file, replace
    83→// - Display names: WriteFile, Edit
    84→const (
    85→	ToolWriteFile = "write_file"
    86→	ToolEditFile  = "edit_file"
    87→	ToolSaveFile  = "save_file"
    88→	ToolReplace   = "replace"
    89→)
    90→
    91→// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files in Gemini CLI
    92→var FileModificationTools = []string{
    93→	ToolWriteFile,
    94→	ToolEditFile,
    95→	ToolSaveFile,
    96→	ToolReplace,
    97→}
    98→
    99→// geminiMessageTokens represents token usage from a Gemini API response.
   100→// This is specific to Gemini's API format where each message has a tokens object.
   101→type geminiMessageTokens struct {
   102→	Input    int `json:"input"`
   103→	Output   int `json:"output"`
   104→	Cached   int `json:"cached"`
   105→	Thoughts int `json:"thoughts"`
   106→	Tool     int `json:"tool"`
   107→	Total    int `json:"total"`
   108→}
   109→
   110→// geminiMessageWithTokens represents a Gemini message with token usage data.
   111→// Used for extracting token counts from Gemini transcripts.
   112→type geminiMessageWithTokens struct {
   113→	ID     string               `json:"id"`
   114→	Type   string               `json:"type"`
   115→	Tokens *geminiMessageTokens `json:"tokens,omitempty"`
   116→}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go",
  "old_string": "// GeminiToolCall represents a tool call in a gemini message",
  "new_string": "// UnmarshalJSON handles both string and array content formats in Gemini transcripts.\n// User messages use: \"content\": [{\"text\": \"...\"}] (array of objects)\n// Gemini messages use: \"content\": \"response text\" (string)\nfunc (m *GeminiMessage) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {\n\t// Use an alias to avoid infinite recursion\n\ttype Alias GeminiMessage\n\taux := &struct {\n\t\tContent json.RawMessage `json:\"content,omitempty\"`\n\t\t*Alias\n\t}{\n\t\tAlias: (*Alias)(m),\n\t}\n\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, aux); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tif len(aux.Content) == 0 || string(aux.Content) == \"null\" {\n\t\tm.Content = \"\"\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Try string first (most common for gemini messages)\n\tvar strContent string\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(aux.Content, &strContent); err == nil {\n\t\tm.Content = strContent\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Try array of objects with \"text\" fields (user messages)\n\tvar parts []struct {\n\t\tText string `json:\"text\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(aux.Content, &parts); err == nil {\n\t\tvar texts []string\n\t\tfor _, p := range parts {\n\t\t\tif p.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\ttexts = append(texts, p.Text)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.Content = strings.Join(texts, \"\\n\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Unknown format - leave content empty\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// GeminiToolCall represents a tool call in a gemini message"
}
```

```
     1→package geminicli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"strings"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// Transcript parsing types - Gemini CLI uses JSON format for session storage
    13→// Based on transcript_path format: ~/.gemini/tmp/<hash>/chats/session-<date>-<id>.json
    14→
    15→// Message type constants for Gemini transcripts
    16→const (
    17→	MessageTypeUser   = "user"
    18→	MessageTypeGemini = "gemini"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// GeminiTranscript represents the top-level structure of a Gemini session file
    22→type GeminiTranscript struct {
    23→	Messages []GeminiMessage `json:"messages"`
    24→}
    25→
    26→// GeminiMessage represents a single message in the transcript
    27→type GeminiMessage struct {
    28→	ID        string           `json:"id,omitempty"` // UUID for the message
    29→	Type      string           `json:"type"`         // MessageTypeUser or MessageTypeGemini
    30→	Content   string           `json:"content,omitempty"`
    31→	ToolCalls []GeminiToolCall `json:"toolCalls,omitempty"`
    32→}
    33→
    34→// UnmarshalJSON handles both string and array content formats in Gemini transcripts.
    35→// User messages use: "content": [{"text": "..."}] (array of objects)
    36→// Gemini messages use: "content": "response text" (string)
    37→func (m *GeminiMessage) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {
    38→	// Use an alias to avoid infinite recursion
    39→	type Alias GeminiMessage
    40→	aux := &struct {
    41→		Content json.RawMessage `json:"content,omitempty"`
    42→		*Alias
    43→	}{
    44→		Alias: (*Alias)(m),
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, aux); err != nil {
    48→		return err
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	if len(aux.Content) == 0 || string(aux.Content) == "null" {
    52→		m.Content = ""
    53→		return nil
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// Try string first (most common for gemini messages)
    57→	var strContent string
    58→	if err := json.Unmarshal(aux.Content, &strContent); err == nil {
    59→		m.Content = strContent
    60→		return nil
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	// Try array of objects with "text" fields (user messages)
    64→	var parts []struct {
    65→		Text string `json:"text"`
    66→	}
    67→	if err := json.Unmarshal(aux.Content, &parts); err == nil {
    68→		var texts []string
    69→		for _, p := range parts {
    70→			if p.Text != "" {
    71→				texts = append(texts, p.Text)
    72→			}
    73→		}
    74→		m.Content = strings.Join(texts, "\n")
    75→		return nil
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// Unknown format - leave content empty
    79→	return nil
    80→}
```

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Changes Made

### `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go`
- Added `strings` import
- Added custom `UnmarshalJSON` method on `GeminiMessage` that handles both content formats:
  - **String**: `"content": "text"` — used as-is
  - **Array**: `"content": [{"text": "..."}]` — extracts text parts, joins with newlines
  - **Null/absent**: leaves content as empty string

### `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript_test.go`
Added 5 new test cases:
- `TestParseTranscript_ArrayContent` — mixed array (user) and string (gemini) content
- `TestParseTranscript_ArrayContentMultipleParts` — multiple text parts joined with newlines
- `TestParseTranscript_NullContent` — null content field
- `TestExtractAllUserPrompts_ArrayContent` — prompt extraction from array format
- `TestExtractModifiedFiles_ArrayContent` — file extraction with array-content user messages
- Added `"array content"` subtest to `TestExtractLastUserPrompt`

### `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_test.go`
- Added `"Gemini JSON with array content (real format)"` case to `TestCountTranscriptItems` — the key regression test that would have caught this bug
- Added `"Gemini array content (real format)"` case to `TestExtractUserPrompts`

The fix is fully backward-compatible — all existing tests continue to pass unchanged. The `UnmarshalJSON` approach means all downstream code accessing `msg.Content` works without any changes.

```
diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go b/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go
index a5d2ab61..36ab5d5d 100644
--- a/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go
@@ -4,6 +4,7 @@ import (
 	"encoding/json"
 	"fmt"
 	"os"
+	"strings"
 
 	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
 )
@@ -30,6 +31,55 @@ type GeminiMessage struct {
 	ToolCalls []GeminiToolCall `json:"toolCalls,omitempty"`
 }
 
+// UnmarshalJSON handles both string and array content formats in Gemini transcripts.
+// User messages use: "content": [{"text": "..."}] (array of objects)
+// Gemini messages use: "content": "response text" (string)
+func (m *GeminiMessage) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {
+	// Use an alias to avoid infinite recursion
+	type Alias GeminiMessage
+	aux := &struct {
+		*Alias
+
+		Content json.RawMessage `json:"content,omitempty"`
+	}{
+		Alias: (*Alias)(m),
+	}
+
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, aux); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal message: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(aux.Content) == 0 || string(aux.Content) == "null" {
+		m.Content = ""
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	// Try string first (most common for gemini messages)
+	var strContent string
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(aux.Content, &strContent); err == nil {
+		m.Content = strContent
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	// Try array of objects with "text" fields (user messages)
+	var parts []struct {
+		Text string `json:"text"`
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(aux.Content, &parts); err == nil {
+		var texts []string
+		for _, p := range parts {
+			if p.Text != "" {
+				texts = append(texts, p.Text)
+			}
+		}
+		m.Content = strings.Join(texts, "\n")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	// Unknown format - leave content empty
+	return nil
+}
+
 // GeminiToolCall represents a tool call in a gemini message
 type GeminiToolCall struct {
 	ID     string                 `json:"id"`

+func TestParseTranscript_ArrayContent(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	// Real Gemini CLI format: user messages have array content, gemini messages have string content
+	data := []byte(`{
+  "messages": [
+    {"type": "user", "content": [{"text": "hello world"}]},
+    {"type": "gemini", "content": "hi there"},
+    {"type": "user", "content": [{"text": "do something"}]},
+    {"type": "gemini", "content": "sure thing"}
+  ]
+}`)
+
+	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(transcript.Messages) != 4 {
+		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() got %d messages, want 4", len(transcript.Messages))
+	}
+
+	// User messages should have extracted text from array
+	if transcript.Messages[0].Content != "hello world" {
+		t.Errorf("Message 0 content = %q, want %q", transcript.Messages[0].Content, "hello world")
+	}
+	if transcript.Messages[2].Content != "do something" {
+		t.Errorf("Message 2 content = %q, want %q", transcript.Messages[2].Content, "do something")
+	}
+
+	// Gemini messages should have string content as-is
+	if transcript.Messages[1].Content != "hi there" {
+		t.Errorf("Message 1 content = %q, want %q", transcript.Messages[1].Content, "hi there")
+	}
+	if transcript.Messages[3].Content != "sure thing" {
+		t.Errorf("Message 3 content = %q, want %q", transcript.Messages[3].Content, "sure thing")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestParseTranscript_ArrayContentMultipleParts(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	// Array content with multiple text parts should be joined with newlines
+	data := []byte(`{
+  "messages": [
+    {"type": "user", "content": [{"text": "part one"}, {"text": "part two"}]}
+  ]
+}`)
+
+	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(transcript.Messages) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() got %d messages, want 1", len(transcript.Messages))
+	}
+
+	want := "part one\npart two"
+	if transcript.Messages[0].Content != want {
+		t.Errorf("Content = %q, want %q", transcript.Messages[0].Content, want)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestParseTranscript_NullContent(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	data := []byte(`{
+  "messages": [
+    {"type": "user", "content": null},
+    {"type": "gemini", "content": "response"}
+  ]
+}`)
+
+	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if transcript.Messages[0].Content != "" {
+		t.Errorf("Content = %q, want empty string", transcript.Messages[0].Content)
+	}
+}
+
```

```
    82→            "content": "# The Color Red\n\nRed is a color at the long-wavelength end of the visible spectrum of light next to orange and opposite violet. It is one of the primary colors in the RGB color model and a secondary color (made from magenta and yellow) in the CMYK color model, and is widely used in art and culture.\n\n## Symbolism\n\nRed is often associated with:\n*   **Love and Passion:** Due to its vibrant and intense nature.\n*   **Danger and Warning:** Commonly used in stop signs and warning signals.\n*   **Energy and Action:** Can evoke feelings of excitement and urgency.\n*   **Anger and Aggression:** In some contexts, it can symbolize strong negative emotions.\n\n## Interesting Facts\n\n*   Red is the color most often associated with heat and fire.\n*   The human eye perceives red more readily than other colors, which is why it's frequently used for important alerts.\n*   Many cultures associate red with good luck and prosperity.\n"
```

> AGENT

Ah, I see the issue. The `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer is NOT separated by a blank line from the subject:

```
docs: Add red.md with information about the color red
    Entire-Checkpoint: 80a24c989e27
```

Git requires trailers to be in a separate paragraph (blank line before them). Without it, git doesn't recognize it as a trailer. Let me look at how the prepare-commit-msg hook adds the trailer.

> AGENT

Found the bug. The `addCheckpointTrailer` function at line 1116 checks if a line contains `": "` to detect existing trailers. But a commit subject like `docs: Add red.md with information about the color red` contains `": "` after "red", so it falsely thinks trailers already exist and skips the blank line.

Let me see which code path the agent commit uses.

> AGENT

It's not Gemini-specific — the bug is in `addCheckpointTrailer` which is shared. It affects any single-line commit message containing `": "` (like conventional commits: `docs:`, `feat:`, `fix:`, etc.). Claude Code just happens to not trigger it because in manual-commit strategy, the USER commits (not the agent), and typically uses multi-line messages or the editor flow.

With Gemini, the agent itself runs `git commit -m "docs: Add red.md..."` — a single-line message where the subject contains `": "`, falsely triggering the "already has trailers" detection.

The fix is straightforward — a single-paragraph message can't have trailers by git's definition. Let me write a test first, then fix it.

```json
  "old_string": "\t// Check if last non-empty, non-comment line looks like a trailer\n\t// Git comment lines start with # and may contain \": \" (e.g., \"# Changes to be committed:\")\n\thasTrailers := false\n\tfor i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tline := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])\n\t\tif line == \"\" {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Skip git comment lines\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(line, \"#\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif strings.Contains(line, \": \") {\n\t\t\thasTrailers = true\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Non-comment, non-trailer line found - no existing trailers\n\t\tbreak\n\t}",
  "new_string": "\t// Check if last non-empty, non-comment line looks like a trailer.\n\t// Git trailers must be in a separate paragraph (after a blank line).\n\t// A single-paragraph message (e.g., just a subject line) cannot have trailers,\n\t// even if the subject contains \": \" (like conventional commits: \"docs: Add foo\").\n\thasTrailers := false\n\tseenBlankLine := false\n\tfor i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tline := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])\n\t\tif line == \"\" {\n\t\t\tseenBlankLine = true\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Skip git comment lines\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(line, \"#\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif strings.Contains(line, \": \") {\n\t\t\thasTrailers = true\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Non-comment, non-trailer line found - no existing trailers\n\t\tbreak\n\t}\n\t// Trailers require a preceding blank line to be a separate paragraph\n\tif !seenBlankLine {\n\t\thasTrailers = false\n\t}"
```
